localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY, s); // Backup dans sessionStorage pour plus de résilience sessionStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY, s); } catch(e) {} } function loadImgs() { try { const raw = localStorage.getItem(IMGS_KEY) || sessionStorage.getItem(IMGS_KEY); if (raw) return JSON.parse(raw); } catch(e) {} return {}; } function saveImgs(imgs) { try { const s = JSON.stringify(imgs); => { const r = new FileReader(); r.onload = e => res(e.target.result); r.onerror = rej; r.readAsDataURL(file); }); } function StoreProvider({ children }) { const savedStore = loadStore(); const = React.useMemo(() => { const completed = orders.filter(o => o.status !== "Annulé"); const revenue = completed.reduce((s, o) => s + (o.total || 0), 0); const clients = new Set(completed.map(o => o.phone || o.clientPhone || o.id)).size; return { total: orders.length, revenue, clients, rating: 0, /* sera implémenté quand les avis seront ajoutés */ }; }, [orders]); const persistAll = (nextInfo, nextDishes, nextPlanJour, nextOrders, nextSub) => { saveStore({ info: nextInfo, dishes: nextDishes, planJour: nextPlanJour, orders: nextOrders, sub: nextSub || sub }); }; const setInfo = (upd) => { setInfoRaw(prev => { const next = typeof upd === "function" ? upd(prev) : { ...prev, ...upd }; saveStore({ info: next, dishes, planJour, orders, sub }); return next; }); }; const setSub = (upd) => { setSubRaw(prev => { const next = typeof upd === "function" ? upd(prev) : { ...prev, ...upd }; saveStore({ info, dishes, planJour, orders, sub: next }); return next; }); }; // Calculate days remaining const subDaysRemaining = React.useMemo(() => { if (!sub.expiryDate) return 14; const diff = new Date(sub.expiryDate) - new Date(); return Math.max(0, Math.ceil(diff / 86400000)); }, [sub.expiryDate]); const setDishes = (upd) => { setDishesRaw(prev => { const next = typeof upd === "function" ? upd(prev) : upd; saveStore({ info, dishes: next, planJour, orders, sub }); return next; }); }; const setPlanJour = (upd) => { setPlanJourRaw(prev => { const next = typeof upd === "function" ? upd(prev) : upd; saveStore({ info, dishes, planJour: next, orders, sub }); return next; }); }; /* ── Ajouter une commande (appelé depuis CheckoutFlow) ──────── */ const addOrder = (order) => { const newOrder = { ...order, id: "#" + (1000 + orders.length + 1), createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), status: "En cours", }; setOrdersRaw(prev => { const next = [newOrder, ...prev]; saveStore({ info, dishes, planJour, orders: next, sub }); return next; }); return newOrder; }; const updateOrderStatus = (id, status) => { setOrdersRaw(prev => { const next = prev.map(o => o.id === id ? { ...o, status } : o); saveStore({ info, dishes, planJour, orders: next, sub }); return next; }); }; const setLogo = async (file) => { if (!file) return; const b64 = await fileToBase64(file); setImgsRaw(prev => { const next = { ...prev, logo: b64 }; saveImgs(next); return next; }); }; const setCover = async (file) => { if (!file) return; const b64 = await fileToBase64(file); setImgsRaw(prev => { const next = { ...prev, cover: b64 }; saveImgs(next); return next; }); }; const setDishImg = async (id, file) => { if (!file) return; const b64 = await fileToBase64(file); const key = `dish_${id}`; setImgsRaw(prev => { const next = { ...prev, [key]: b64 }; saveImgs(next); return next; }); setDishesRaw(prev => { const next = prev.map(d => d.id === id ? { ...d, img: b64 } : d); saveStore({ info, dishes: next, planJour, orders, sub }); return next; }); }; const getDishImg = (id, fallback) => imgs[`dish_${id}`] || fallback; return ( {children} ); } function useStore() { return useContext(StoreCtx); } /* ── HOOK UPLOAD (local, sans persistance — pour cas temporaires) ── */ function useUpload(initial = null) { const [src, setSrc] = useState(initial); const ref = useRef(null); const trigger = () => ref.current?.click(); const Input = () => ( { const f = e.target.files?.[0]; if(f) setSrc(URL.createObjectURL(f)); e.target.value=""; }}/> ); return { src, setSrc, trigger, Input }; } /* ── HOOK UPLOAD PERSISTANT ──────────────────────────────────────── */ function usePersistUpload(storeKey, initial = null) { const ref = useRef(null); const trigger = () => ref.current?.click(); const Input = ({ onFile }) => ( { const f = e.target.files?.[0]; if(f && onFile) onFile(f); e.target.value=""; }}/> ); return { trigger, Input }; } /* ── GLOBAL STYLES ───────────────────────────────────────────────── */ function GS() { return ( ); } /* ── ICÔNES SVG ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── */ img: , lock: , layout: , map: , phone: , copy: , dl: , share: , clock: , store: , palette:, wa: , fb: , ig: , filter: , bar: , info: , }; return icons[n] || null; } /* ── LOGOS PAIEMENT — vrais logos
